# Correspondence between Lantana-Rocq and Lantana-Lean

Generated by `scripts/rocq-table.sh` (`scripts/RocqTable.lean`) from the environment of `import Lantana`; do not edit by hand. A Lean declaration carrying `@[lantana_alias rocqName]` reimplements the Rocq declaration `rocqName` (and `#check RocqLantana.rocqName` finds it); `#lantana_ignore rocqName "reason"` records a Rocq declaration deliberately not reimplemented (`Lantana/Std/Porting.lean`).

Rocq reference: branch `main`, commit `08838e2`.

## Summary

- **2302** Rocq names aliased (`@[lantana_alias]`), to 2258 Lean declarations in 87 Lean modules.
- **490** Rocq names ignored (`#lantana_ignore`), recorded in 47 Lean modules.
- Coverage: the scanner finds **3586** declarations (definitions, lemmas, instances, inductives, records and classes with their constructors and fields) in 102 Rocq files of `lantana/` and `lantana_heap_lang/` (and 266 section-local `Let`s, notations, tactics and module aliases, matched but not counted): **2284** aliased, **483** ignored, **819** neither (section 3).
- 0 aliases and 0 ignores name no declaration the scanner finds; they are listed at the end of sections 1 and 2. 71 names match declarations of several Rocq files and are resolved by the Lean module's directory and name.

The scanner and the name resolution are heuristic (see `scripts/RocqTable.lean`).
